run occurred during this audit. `/root/review_b4b_result_protocol` completed
independent read-only review and corrected its initial resolution inference;
full-history fork, model/effort inherited with exact settings unverified.

Decision: retain **96/100 observed, schedule/resource/video verified, native
action-contract qualification outstanding**. Do not discard successful
integration evidence or relabel it as strict protocol/published-score parity.
The minimal next work is an explicitly scoped short per-dimension
policy-action/controller-boundary probe and clarification of native saturation
versus additional clipping, before freezing a clean/noisy comparison contract.
Do not rerun 100 episodes just because the unused config field says 360. No
automatic retraining, noise evaluation or baseline checkpoint replacement.

## Short Native Action Boundary Probe (2026-09-12)

The user approved only the short diagnostic probe. SSH was reachable and the
GPU was idle before execution. New files are
`tools/probe_pi05_libero_action_boundary.py`,
`tools/test_pi05_libero_action_boundary.py`, and
`tools/render_pi05_libero_action_boundary.py`. Small files were sequentially
synced and SHA256-matched before remote use. All 12 CPU tests passed locally
and remotely; ten real boundary checks passed. No training, checkpoint change,
noise evaluation or full-suite rerun occurred.

The probe reused the official evaluator's factories, seed1000, pinned
checkpoint/config/processors, compiled precision settings, chunk50,
inference10 and n_action_steps10. It selected task0/init0 and checked the
actual payload hash against B4b's recorded task0/seed1000 state. Its loop
stopped after20 steps while the native horizon remained280; resource/load/run
time was67.952s. Only one environment was constructed, versus B4b's ten-task
factory sequence: this is a mechanism probe, not an exact B4b trajectory replay.

| Native environment-input dimension | Minimum | Maximum | Outside [-1,1] / 20 |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| delta_x | 0.032930 | 0.953075 | 0 |
| delta_y | 0.048063 | 0.504059 | 0 |
| delta_z | -0.709145 | 0.171555 | 0 |
| delta_rx | -0.042405 | 0.011293 | 0 |
| delta_ry | -0.024064 | 0.051503 | 0 |
| delta_rz | -0.000922 | 0.003291 | 0 |
| gripper | -1.009158 | -0.990337 | 8 |

All20 postprocessed7D actions reached the inner environment unchanged. Each
policy step generated one OSC scaling call and25 native gripper formatting
calls (500 total), all independently checked against copied inputs/outputs.
The original methods were called once and returned unchanged; no clipped
action was fed back by the logger. OSC scales translation to +/-0.05 and
rotation to +/-0.5; no arm saturation occurred in this window. Native source
clipping exists, but its arm-clipping branch was not numerically exercised
by an out-of-bounds arm command here. Gripper uses sign(input) and a clipped
internal accumulator. The8 slight negative overshoots thus retain the same
open direction as other negative inputs, not greater opening magnitude.
This does not diagnose the four failed B4b episodes or establish that all100
historical episodes only overshot in the gripper dimension.

Actual20-step raw image tensors were `[1,256,256,3]` for both views; policy

## Fixed Paired Low-Contrast Protocol And Entrypoint (2026-09-12)

User approved the new native-controller comparison. The separate contract is
`docs/libero-spatial-low-contrast-protocol-v1.json`, SHA256
`11dbb573db0bab86e883863ec38f0721f695bb345ad037b845c2bbd37d06bf72`.
The old frozen score protocol, B4b runner/result and boundary probe remain
unchanged. New finite native7 raw overshoots are recorded per dimension, not
silently clipped or treated as an automatic failure; original OSC clipping/
scaling and Panda sign/internal accumulation stay intact. This is an explicit
new evaluation contract, not a retrospective strict-pass label for B4b.

The experiment-planning skill's checklist was applied to the protocol (fixed
baseline, pairing, metrics, efficiency and limitations). This is an engineering
evaluation handoff, not a manuscript Results/Discussion chapter; no mock results
or paper-section/table placeholders were created.

Fixed design:

- Domain: existing public LIBERO Spatial environment/assets, no demonstration
  download, project data inspection, dataset split or training.
- `check`: task0/init0/seed1000, clean then low_contrast, two full episodes.
- `screen`: tasks0..9, init0/seed1000 each, clean then low_contrast,20 NEW
  full episodes; do not reuse check episodes or historical B4b scores.
- Corruption: only two raw256x256 RGB views, existing low_contrast severity2,
  alpha0.45 about each channel's spatial mean, float32 then uint8 rounding.
  No blur/occlusion combination, severity sweep or outcome-driven tuning.
  This is a controlled visibility perturbation, not measured vessel oxidation.
- Same pinned PI0.5/config/processors, native280 horizon,10 settling steps,
  one synchronous environment per task, chunk50/inference10/n_action_steps10.
  Controller class, dimensions, limits and gripper speed checked at runtime.
- Reset queue and global Python/NumPy/Torch RNG to seed1000 before EACH arm.
  Compare actual init payload, full raw post-settle observation, and actual
  pre-first-inference RNG fingerprints. Changed action trajectories after the
  visual intervention are expected; only starts, not later states, must match.
- Success counts/rates, signed paired delta in percentage points (low-clean),
  relative drop (null if clean zero), four paired outcomes and per-task results.
  Equal task weighting means macro and pooled success agree. No significance
  claim or robust population estimate from one initialization per task.
- Runtime is logged for scheduling; fixed clean-first order/compile warm-up
  does not support comparative inference-speed claims. No architecture module,
  loss or world-model ablation is tested, so no innovation gain/XAI claim.

## Task4 Reset-Only Diagnosis After Screen Failure (2026-09-12)

The user-run `pi05_libero_low_contrast_screen_v1` failed after10 completed
episodes with `Unpaired actual start: task 4 initial_observation_sha256`.
Tasks0–3 paired; task4's two episodes succeeded but their initial uncorrupted
images and first-inference RNG differed. No report/full-screen score exists.
Do not reuse these partial outcomes or rerun into the existing directory.

The user authorized only a reset-only diagnosis. New algorithm-side files:

```text
tools/probe_libero_task4_reset.py
  sha256=715a44e3dbbe068f6897307fc265cd3f7519bc429a30a43b2474d88410d157b8
tools/test_libero_task4_reset_probe.py
  sha256=ab3fc4c450b6f6a0014d33d96e4b069d0895ffb2ead170b1251f15e3b2fcf941
simulation_output/libero_task4_reset_probe_v1/report.json
  sha256=333dff2e45beffa6775ab9be97c2fb3e3903cbde19c6fb1ceb7a1ace04277eb2
```

Five helper tests passed locally and remotely. All final small sources were
sequentially uploaded and SHA-matched. Native probe completed in15.182s:
three measured resets on one shared environment, then one measured reset on
each of two fresh environments; task4/init0/seed1000 throughout. The50 measured
settling steps exclude uninstrumented constructor activity. No policy loaded,
policy action, corruption, optimizer step, checkpoint write or full evaluation.
All218 protected historical B4b/check/failed-screen files stayed byte-identical.

Observed findings:

- Identical seeds and init payload do NOT make repeated native resets identical.
  `shared_0` differs from both `shared_1` and `shared_2`; `fresh_1` and `fresh_2`
  match `shared_0` at every measured stage, including all recorded state leaves,
  raw views and component RNG fingerprints.
- Earliest sampled divergence: native reset return, before fixed init loading.
  NumPy RNG differs; Python/Torch CPU/CUDA remain equal. All five measured
  resets used exactly one native attempt; no RandomizationError occurred.
- After identical `set_init_state`, qpos/qvel match, but model body positions
  at indices28/33 and body quaternion at28 remain different; both raw images
  differ immediately. Static model placement is not restored by the flattened
  MuJoCo init payload. Later settling retains these placement/image differences.
- `shared_0` raw formatted start hash is
  `01fcb5b2f497ba00a786c94d6a1aefbb7500d08c3d003cf2f3fa729c0f9bffa0`,
  exactly the failed screen's task4 clean start. `shared_2` is
  `5cab2d000c71ee6af3502892886816d00ea1cdcddd7f4051cdf52fef3debebbb`,
  exactly its low-contrast arm's PRE-corruption start. Thus the mismatching
  starts can be reproduced without PI05 inference or its preceding trajectory.

The observed cause is reset lifecycle/state incompleteness, not the image
corruption or model loading. The precise internal initializer branch responsible
for different NumPy consumption is not yet traced. The installed LIBERO
`bddl_base_domain.py` resets fixture poses through `sim.model.body_pos/body_quat`,
whereas `env_wrapper.py` restores init through `set_state_from_flattened`.
No installed dependency was patched; native retry/control semantics remain intact.

Observer snapshots copy original returned observations and fresh runtime
references; no extra render, forward or observation refresh is inserted.
Per-attempt RNG records are persisted immediately; snapshots assert that their
own capture did not advance global RNG. Complete numeric arrays and rawimages
are diagnostic-only sidecars, never policy inputs.

Decision: keep screen blocked. A candidate minimal repair is a separately
versioned environment lifecycle with identically seeded fresh native environments
for both arms, followed by strict init/raw-observation/first-inference RNG checks.
This requires updating the declared lifecycle protocol, not weakening equality
or silently editing the frozen v1 contract. This turn did not implement that
repair, run PI05 checks, replay a trajectory or rerun screen. Task4 evidence
does not yet establish all-task lifecycle consistency or repaired model scoring.

Independent reviewer `/root/review_b4b_result_protocol` completed read-only
review; original full-history fork, model/effort inherited and exact values
unverified. Its snapshot-label, retry-persistence and measured-step-count
findings were addressed before the native run. Root owns execution/evidence.

Root directly viewed six raw two-view images at `after_set_init_state` for
shared_0/shared_2/fresh_2. Fixture placement/orientation differs in the reused
environment; fresh_2 and shared_0 images are byte-identical. Exact PNG hashes
and scope are recorded in the probe's `visual_review.json`, status
`viewed_not_accepted` (no explicit user visual approval). These are raw
pre-native-flip reset images, not terminal or policy-success evidence.
Only a340808byte review archive was mirrored locally (report/status/manifest
and six PNGs); the complete stage JSON/NPZ remains remote. A slower complete
archive transfer was stopped and its partial local file retained, not verified
or extracted. Review archive SHA256 is
`a4cd9dc33cca9f3cb93c24aa303cf147dc2a89f5c41eee893f055e79b80ce3fa`.

## Native LIBERO World-Model Forward Interface (2026-09-12)

User approved the next native7D/task forward interface and zero-optimizer-step
checks. SSH succeeded. New code is separate from PI0.5 and the guidewire world
model; no backbone, mixed head, existing loss/adapter/trainer, shared interface,
collector, data or SOFA file was changed. No full training or policy evaluation.

Implementation:

```text
tools/pi05_libero_world_model.py
tools/test_pi05_libero_world_model.py
tools/smoke_pi05_libero_world_model.py
tools/test_pi05_libero_world_model_forward_smoke.py
```

`LiberoWorldModel` consumes only the existing native window's six input fields:
dual-view history `[B,4,2,2048]`, visual boolean mask, normalized state history
`[B,4,8]`, state boolean mask, exact task instruction list and already-normalized
candidate actions `[B,K,3,7]`. Targets and provenance metadata are never passed
to it. The collator rejects extra keys, guidewire9D/32D shapes, numeric masks,
unknown/missing actions and nonfinite observed values; it copies input storage.

Ordered per-view projections plus state/masks feed a128-wide context GRU.
The exact registered instruction maps to a closed-set task embedding, fused
with history to initialize an independent unidirectional action GRU for each
candidate. It returns only:

- `pred_future_visual_latent`: `[B,K,3,2,2048]`, current masked visual latent
  plus a predicted residual; no pixel decoder.
- `pred_state_delta`: `[B,K,3,8]`, normalized current-relative coordinate
  residual, not SO(3) relative rotation or a guidewire rotation target.

Invalid NaN/Inf values are cleared with `where` BEFORE projections and the
current-visual residual anchor. Masks themselves enter the context. Actions
are neither imputed nor clipped to[-1,1] after normalization. Registered text
must match exactly; source/task IDs remain metadata and only determine the
stable internal lookup table. This is closed-set conditioning, not a pretrained
language encoder or evidence of semantic/open-language grounding.

The actual CPU smoke locks hidden128, history4, horizon3, two2048-wide views,
state8/action7, dropout0, seed20260912 and batch16. The module itself supports
other positive history/horizon/feature/hidden sizes for synthetic tests; native
state/action/view dimensions remain fixed. Future explicitly approved training
could reuse forward; the smoke explicitly sets eval/frozen/inference mode.
No native loss, consistency objective, ranking rule or optimizer was added.

## Native LIBERO Masked Objectives And Zero-Step Entrypoint (2026-09-12)

User approved native loss/metrics and a dry-run-only entrypoint, explicitly
without backward or optimization. SSH succeeded. Only four new algorithm files
were added; existing native model/adapter, PI0.5 backbone, mixed head, guidewire
world model/loss/trainer, shared interface, collectors and data/SOFA files remain
unchanged. This is a numerical interface milestone, not a training result.

```text
tools/pi05_libero_world_model_objectives.py
tools/test_pi05_libero_world_model_objectives.py
tools/train_pi05_libero_world_model.py
tools/test_pi05_libero_world_model_dry_run.py
```

Fixed objective: `L = masked_mean(SmoothL1_visual) + 0.25 *
masked_mean(SmoothL1_normalized_state)`, beta1. Only the single recorded executed
candidate K1 is supervised; no candidate averaging, ranking, uncertainty,
consistency or extra target head. This new objective does not alter the old
guidewire loss. Four native targets are separately collated; they are never
forwarded as model inputs. Unknown/diagnostic/metadata fields, numeric masks,
shape broadcasting and target gradients are rejected. Both predictions and
targets at invalid locations are cleared with `where` before arithmetic;
observed nonfinite values are rejected.

Each branch uses its own effective scalar count: valid visual horizon/view
positions times2048, and valid state horizon/coordinate positions. An empty
branch contributes loss0; no positively weighted support fails closed.
Elementwise SmoothL1 is float32, with float64 sums/count division and float32
branch/scalar losses. Across batches the runner separately sums branch totals
and counts, then weights their global means; it never averages batch means.
Metric errors are detached and promoted to float64 BEFORE subtraction, then
accumulated in window order; their arithmetic is intentionally distinct from
the float32 elementwise loss. Missing MAE/RMSE are null, not zero.

Metrics cover visual latent MAE/RMSE by horizon/view and normalized state by
horizon/coordinate. Native state errors use only multiplication by the verified
train-only state_std, without adding means. They are reported per coordinate,
per horizon/coordinate and within position, axis-angle-coordinate and gripper
groups; no mixed-unit all-coordinate native MAE. Axis-angle coordinate error
is not SO(3) rotation error. Constant-valid std1 remains the old normalizer's
numerical fallback; a train-unsupported coordinate remains masked by the old
adapter. No validation-derived scale or new normalization was introduced.

The entrypoint requires `--dry-run --max-steps 0 --device cpu`. Missing dry-run,
nonzero/noninteger steps or a non-CPU device are rejected before creating an
output or accessing source artifacts. There is no optimizer implementation or
alternative training branch. Source report SHA, all33 feature artifact hashes
and frozen extraction provenance are verified before heavy model imports.
It reuses the fixed1400/1402 episode split, train140 state/139 action rows,
model D2048/hidden128/T4/H3/state8/action7/views2, seed20260912, batch16 and eval/
frozen/inference mode. Only the existing six inputs reach forward.

## Native Persistence Baseline And Frozen Learning Diagnostic Protocol (2026-09-12)

User approved the persistence reference and training/evaluation protocol, not
optimization. SSH succeeded; four new algorithm Python files and one protocol
JSON were sequentially SCP-synchronized and SHA-verified before remote CPU
execution. Existing model, adapter, objectives, dry-run trainer, guidewire
models, shared contracts, data/SOFA files and weekly log were not changed.

```text
tools/pi05_libero_world_model_persistence.py
tools/test_pi05_libero_world_model_persistence.py
tools/eval_pi05_libero_world_model_persistence.py
tools/test_pi05_libero_world_model_persistence_eval.py
docs/libero-native-world-model-learning-protocol-v1.json
```

Persistence predicts the latest observed two-view visual latent at all three
future steps and a zero8D normalized current-relative state residual. It has
no parameters and ignores action/task values after validating the native six
input fields. Targets remain separate. Common scoring support is original
target-valid AND latest-observation-valid, with no fallback to older frames.
The helper validates original declared-valid targets before intersecting masks.
The fixed pack has identical original/common support; every batch's masks,
input/target tensor hashes and metadata matched the previous random-model
trace exactly. A mismatch rejects reference reuse rather than changing masks
or silently recomputing a different comparison. No model was constructed or
rerun; the old random report is hash-bound read-only evidence.

Local/remote163/163 tests passed (previous128 +23 persistence +12 evaluation
guards); the remote full301-window baseline completed in0.807216s. All134 train
and167 validation windows were used without shuffle/truncation, same9/11
batches and train-only scales. New tests cover storage ownership, action/task
independence, invalid values, strict K1/native layout, exact-mask rather than
count-only matching, reference hashes, zero-denominator comparison and sampler
RNG isolation. No backward, optimizer, learned-model forward, feature extraction
or policy action occurred.

Descriptive errors on the previously inspected validation episode1402:

| Metric | Random untrained | Persistence | Absolute change | Relative change |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Normalized8D state MAE | 0.16124926 | 0.08755677 | -0.07369249 | -45.70% |
| Visual latent MAE | 0.15137058 | 0.08917596 | -0.06219463 | -41.09% |
| Masked objective | 0.02658011 | 0.01313894 | -0.01344117 | -50.57% |

Changes are persistence minus random; smaller error is better. Native state
still has separate coordinates/horizons/same-unit groups, never a mixed-unit
aggregate or SO(3) metric. Detailed train/validation errors, all horizons/views/
coordinates and effective counts are in the report. The random visual model

The new helper captures the actual `visual_residual_head` output using one
temporary read-only hook during the same forward. It verifies exact float32
`total = latest visual skip + actual residual`, frozen parameter state/versions,
input bytes and complete hook registry restoration, including exceptions. It
does not subtract total-minus-skip to estimate the residual. Component drift
reductions promote operands to float64 before subtraction; total/residual/skip
use identical broadcast dimensions, while historical input drift is separate.
Their absolute norms are not additive causal shares.

Decision: prioritize a minimal visual-projection conditioning repair hypothesis
before adding a complex robustness module. The recorded bottleneck is near-
universal saturation at the first visual tanh, not a universally saturated
history/context/action pathway. Large raw coordinate offsets and increased
projection preactivation scale are evidence supporting the hypothesis; neither
normalization's benefit nor the training dynamics causing this has been tested.

rejection of the numerical mechanism finding. The primary visual-MAE guard
fails even though state and combined objective improve. Keep both diagnostic
variants and retain the original baseline as the reference; do not promote
the normalized checkpoints as an overall replacement. No extra training,
floor sweep, activation replacement or capacity upgrade follows automatically.

during training and where the visual-MAE regression occurs. No unique cause,
robustness gain, architecture innovation, formal-readiness or real-system
effect has been established.

### Read-Only Renewed Saturation / Visual-Error Diagnosis (2026-09-13)

The user approved the preceding bounded diagnosis only. No new training,
backward, optimizer replay, normalization refit, activation/loss change,
capacity upgrade, PI0.5 backbone operation, feature encoding or rollout was
performed. Project skill maintained the algorithm-only boundary; the
experiment-planning skill fixed the diagnostic groups before actual results.
This is an engineering diagnosis, not a new benchmark or manuscript experiment.

New entrypoint `tools/run_pi05_libero_normalization_diagnosis.py` and helpers
`pi05_libero_normalization_trace_diagnosis.py` / `pi05_libero_projection_geometry.py`
are separate from all frozen prior code. Plan:
`docs/libero-normalization-diagnosis-plan-v1.json`, SHA
`9c2e938a9655c89116b0479c4d659d3b984c05e934208fe601bd48619aa1ec37`.

in2.958s and remotely in2.001s. Independent source review found no remaining
blocker after all original history/state/visual masks were explicitly required
valid. SSH passed,7 small final code/protocol files were sequentially SCP'd and

The first actual diagnosis completed in11.467436s, excluding prior audit and
preflight (estimated1-3minutes). It read2400 old update records and12 final200
checkpoints, reconstructed initial parameters from the original seed and exact
initial-parameter SHA, and made396 frozen clean forward batches. All original
prediction hashes and48 per-episode scores replayed exactly. No intermediate
checkpoint exists and no training was replayed to invent one. All model/input
bytes stayed unchanged;0 backward/optimizer updates/encodings/rollout steps.
Runtime: torch2.10.0+cu128, CPU float32,1 thread, deterministic, no AMP/TF32/CUDA
initialization. Training logs retain the fixed1-20/21-50/51-100/101-150/151-200
intervals; their varying sampled batches are not fixed-set learning curves.

**Finding1: coordinate-wise input normalization does not control the learned
projection's correlated-feature amplification.** On actual float32 normalized
complete train rows, coordinate-mean RMS is about1e-7 and coordinate-std RMS
about1, so the applied normalization has not stopped working. New float64
affine diagnostics use each unique row once (1134 train/524 validation); they
are not the overlapping-window-weighted float32 tanh statistics above.
Observed-action3-seed means, view0/view1 respectively:

| Unique-row partition / quantity | Initial | Final200 |
|---|---:|---:|
|Train / pre-tanh affine RMS|0.5880 / 0.5823|6.0178 / 5.7644|
|Validation / pre-tanh affine RMS|0.6312 / 0.6561|5.5286 / 5.6102|
|Projection weight Frobenius norm|6.5282 / 6.5316|8.1752 / 8.7408|
|Train / centered energy divided by diagonal-covariance reference|1.0382 / 1.0172|69.3580 / 55.6793|
|Validation / same centered-energy ratio|1.0131 / 0.9972|51.4693 / 41.5213|

The weight norm grows only about25%/34%, while affine RMS grows about10x on
train rows. The signed off-diagonal covariance contribution therefore matters:
learned weight directions coherently combine correlated coordinates, not just
uniformly enlarge all weights. This is an algebraic description of the saved
endpoints, not causal shares or proof that every such direction is harmful.
Train final mean-term RMS is only0.01477/0.01521 versus centered RMS
6.01781/5.76437. Validation has an additional mean-shift term1.59294/1.71638,
but centered RMS5.29337/5.33908 still dominates; this is not validation shift
alone. Across the6 primary seed/view endpoints, bias-change RMS is
0.00654-0.00843 versus X-delta-weight RMS5.38874-5.65559.

All2400 old global gradient norms were below0.06643, so the norm1 clip never
engaged. Normalized primary late-step151-200 mean projection-weight gradient
norms remain0.000216-0.000248 (view0) /0.000399-0.000416 (view1), with no exact
zero step in either view. No global gradient explosion or fully dead normalized
projection is evidenced. Small gradients do not exclude cumulative adaptive
optimizer movement and cannot identify when saturation arose. Do not label
this insufficient model capacity, prove saturation caused the MAE regression,
or infer branch-specific gradient dominance from aggregate norms.

**Finding2: average state-loss improvement offsets a small visual-loss
regression; the visual error change is not uniform across horizons.** Original
clean equal-episode macros, then3-seed means, observed_action only:

| Objective component | Baseline | Normalized | Signed delta |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
|Visual SmoothL1|0.0070726195|0.0070878869|+0.0000152673|
|0.25 x state SmoothL1|0.0004818081|0.0004157709|-0.0000660372|
|Total|0.0075544277|0.0075036578|-0.0000507699|

Average visual SmoothL1 increases0.2159%, while state SmoothL1 decreases13.7061%.
Visual-loss deltas by seed are+0.00005010/-0.00003914/+0.00003484: do not say
every seed worsens visual SmoothL1 (all3 do worsen visual MAE). This is exact
objective arithmetic, not a causal claim that the state branch stole gradients.
The zero-action arm is separate and improves both mean loss components; it
does not establish that actions are harmful or generalize the primary finding.

| Future step / visual MAE | Baseline | Normalized | Relative change |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
|1|0.05478156|0.05645034|+3.0462%|
|2|0.07776583|0.07785776|+0.1182%|
|3|0.09990158|0.09957537|-0.3265%|

View0/view1 mean visual MAE changes+0.00029639/+0.00065994; both views regress
at step1. Saved per-view/per-horizon/per-episode MAE and RMSE are reported,
but SmoothL1 breakdown is aggregate plus shared error bins only, not a
per-view/per-horizon loss decomposition. Earlier episode-specific evidence
remains: visual seed-mean improvement only on1458, regressions on1476/1530/1566.

Shared bins are selected using the same seed/arm baseline absolute scalar
error: [0,.01), [.01,.05), [.05,.1), [.1,.5), [.5,1), [1,+inf).
Their primary equal-episode/seed macro contributions to total MAE change are
+0.001337941, +0.000633573, -0.000645126, -0.000802941, -0.000040574,
-0.000004708, summing to+0.000478164. Counts across the3 seeds are
2083910/7000718/4762506/4471464/104673/8729 scalar predictions; these are not
independent samples. Small baseline-error coordinates get worse on average,
offset partly by larger-error-coordinate improvements. Baseline conditioning
permits regression-to-mean; this is neither a denoising benefit nor an oracle
training weight. Empty-bin support/nulls are retained in individual reports.

Decision unchanged: keep the original baseline reference and preserve both
diagnostic variants. Input normalization genuinely alleviates saturation, but
does not yield stable overall visual/state improvement on the reused4-episode
development set. A possible next **proposal**, requiring separate authorization,
is a single-factor projection-output scale-control test before tanh, without
capacity expansion or simultaneous loss retuning; first test its interface and
numerics, then a separately authorized equal-budget comparison with separate
visual/state/horizon guards. Do not implement or train it from this diagnosis
authorization. Endpoint evidence cannot establish onset, unique causation,
policy robustness, independent-test gain, architecture innovation, guidewire
formal-readiness or real-system benefit.

### Projection-Output Scale Control: Interface / Zero-Update Gate (2026-09-13)

The user's next approval authorized implementing/checking the proposed local
scale control. Scope was explicitly staged to interface/numerics first, not a
full training comparison. Project skill preserves algorithm ownership and
short remote verification; experiment-planning skill fixes the one-factor
protocol before actual smoke. No PI0.5 backbone, mixed-head, dataset, label,
loss, capacity, shared policy interface or collection change.

New `tools/pi05_libero_projection_scale_control.py` wraps only the two native
LIBERO visual Linears. Keep the existing train-only input normalization, then
apply `u = z / sqrt(1 + mean(z**2, dim=-1, keepdim=True))` to each Linear output
before the original tanh. The mean uses only that sample/frame/view's hidden
coordinates, not other frames, samples, candidates, or views. There is no
centering, learned affine, running/batch statistics, fitted scale, epsilon
sweep or validation-tuned constant. Fixed1 gives mathematical vector RMS
`r/sqrt(1+r*r) <= 1`; float32 rounding is checked with tolerance1e-6.

The stable implementation computes square/mean/sqrt/division in float64 and
returns float32, retaining autograd. Independent review recommended this before
execution to avoid finite float32 inputs overflowing when squared. Nonfinite
inputs/intermediates fail closed. The native forward still consumes/returns
float32; masked raw skip, targets, state/actions, task lookup, original tanh,
GRUs and SmoothL1 loss remain unchanged. The operator deliberately compresses
amplitude information: bounded vector RMS neither bounds each coordinate below
the tanh saturation threshold nor guarantees nonvanishing/representable gradients.
It is a numerical hypothesis, not a claimed architecture innovation.

`ProjectionScaledLiberoWorldModel` keeps all23 parameter names/order and
1,321,352 parameter elements, adds no RNG draws or trainable parameters, and
does not share parameter storage with the paired reference. New schema
`pi05_libero_projection_scale_control_world_model_v1` and persistent scalar int64
`projection_scale_control_version=1` distinguish the computation. Strict state
loading rejects old/missing/tampered markers, non-strict/assign loading and
constructor-inconsistent visual normalization buffers. Synthetic state_dict
roundtrip is covered; a new trained checkpoint envelope/serializer is NOT yet
implemented and old trained checkpoints must not be relabeled or silently loaded.

New zero-update entrypoint `tools/run_pi05_libero_projection_scale_control.py`
has only preflight/smoke stages, no training switch. Final protocol:
`docs/libero-projection-scale-control-smoke-plan-v1.json`, SHA
`8976c32a2761c512220d1b6d102f0340411617cf213b9c4d1a48a9545b4aebe8`.
It pairs input-normalized versus input-normalized-plus-projection-scaled models
at the original fresh step0 for3 seeds and both action arms. Each seed uses only
its existing first shared16-window training batch, unchanged targets/masks;
the existing1086/500 split is retained. No new validation prediction, normalization
fit, model selection, trained checkpoint load/save, feature encoding or rollout.
All6 input-normalized references must exactly replay the prior normalization
smoke's draws, input/target hashes, initial parameters, predictions, activation
statistics and complete no-update gradient reports.

baseline is an additional reference, not a third newly trained variant.
Desaturation is descriptive and does not establish prediction quality. Even a
paired gate pass does not automatically promote a checkpoint, establish gains
against raw baseline, statistical significance, independent generalization,
robustness, architectural innovation, policy benefit or real-system validity.

original raw baseline, the scaled variant still worsens visual MAE+0.50982%
and h1 visual MAE+2.56332%, while state MAE improves4.54899%. Retain raw baseline,
old normalized variant and new scaled diagnostic artifacts separately. Do not
promote this variant, expand its budget, retune loss/scale or upgrade capacity
from this evidence. A new hypothesis/authorization is needed for further work;
the current numerical hypothesis is tested, not a demonstrated policy gain.

   86-test gate and matched2400-update comparison above. Final tanh saturation
   drops to0.68%/0.43%, but primary improvements are small/inconsistent and both
   h3 guards fail; raw-baseline visual MAE is still better. Retain all artifacts
   without checkpoint promotion. No further training, loss retuning, floor sweep
   or capacity upgrade follows automatically; choose a new bounded hypothesis
   separately rather than rerunning the completed numerical comparison.
   Do not repeat the completed acquisition or feature extraction.
   Do not start
   further optimization or bypass the old fixed-pair gates. This study is not
   family-held-out generalization evidence.
   Source/feature/training gates remain separate; project sim/current-real
   packs still require their independent Gate A2.
5. Retain the fresh paired PI0.5 baseline and the old E2 no-go evidence; do not
   tune CABD/DABC.
6. Keep tactile/contact, rotation, formal-readiness, and real-system claims
   disabled.

## Do Not Do

- Do not continue tuning the collapsed v1 head.
- Do not continue the current `prefix_state` fusion without a new hypothesis;
  equal branch width did not beat `state_only`.
- Do not treat the lower aggregate six-dimensional MAE as translation success;
  most of the gain came from zero-label rotation dims.
- Do not inject tactile context before the state-conditioned Elite baseline has
  completed held-out open-loop evaluation.
- Do not treat 4096 adjacent frames as 4096 independent control decisions; the
  current split has strong action persistence and a partial final episode.
- Do not use the failed real pilot pose audit as training data; it supports only
  the fixed-orientation/translation-target decision.
- Do not optimize or select Elite policies on zero rotation dims `3:6`; retain
  them only for compatibility with existing six-dimensional fields.
- Do not run a full state-conditioning ablation before the readiness gate in
  `docs/vla-training-data-readiness-audit.md` is reviewed.
- Do not save or continue the original 4096-record translation-only run; it did
  not beat that dataset's six-dimensional Elite-only run. The new stride-5 scan
  is a separate controlled baseline and must use its fixed manifest split.
- Do not present the current random-base checkpoint as pretrained PI05.
- Do not modify data collectors, simulator experts, or real hardware code.
- Do not redefine Piper labels to make the classifier easier.
- Do not feed exact MuJoCo contact, wall, route, or tip truth into the policy.
- Do not use `diagnostic_targets.jsonl` as an adapter observation or target.
- Do not pass the contact-risk diagnostic switch in training commands; it does
  not promote `policy_input_allowed=false` fields.
- Do not run long training or full rendered evaluation inside the agent session
  without explicit user direction.
- Do not write algorithm experiment detail into `docs/data-track-handoff.md`.
- Do not use E2 records for validation, early stopping, or model selection.
- Do not expose `training_weight`, `sample_role`, or diagnostic targets as
  policy observations or action targets.
- Do not train on the historical senior real dataset or use it for current
  normalization, validation, or model selection.
- Do not count passive-left branch entry as guidance-valid success.
- Do not start action-effect optimization before Gates A1 and A2 in
  `docs/vla-action-effect-world-model-plan.md` pass.

## Documentation Ownership

Algorithm-only durable changes go here. New reusable algorithm commands go in
`docs/algorithm-track-commands.md`. Shared interface changes also require a
small update to `docs/project-state.md` or `docs/handoff.md` and coordination
with the data track.

Group-meeting facts from either track go into the shared append-only
`docs/weekly-meeting-log.md`.
